Re-read this block before you\nstart writing code and again before you finish.\n\nH1. **FILES THAT MUST EXIST.** You must create exactly these three files and\n    every one of them must be non-empty and syntactically valid JavaScript/HTML:\n        - `index.html`\n        - `elevator.js`\n        - `person.js`\n    `index.html` references `elevator.js` and `person.js` via `<script src=...>`.\n    If either .js file is missing, the browser will log `404 Not Found` and the\n    page will be blank. Before finishing, use your file-read tool to open each\n    of the three files and visually confirm they contain complete code.\n\nH2. **NO ES6 MODULES. NO `import`. NO `export`.** This is a plain-script\n    project using global variables. Writing `import { createPerson } from ...`\n    or `export function ...` will produce the console error\n    `SyntaxError: Cannot use import statement outside a module` and the whole\n    simulation will fail to start. Communicate between files using global\n    functions/variables attached to `window` or declared at the top level. Do\n    not add `type=\"module\"` to any `<script>` tag.\n\nH3. **THE SIMULATION MUST AUTO-START ON PAGE LOAD.** At the bottom of\n    `elevator.js`, after all functions are declared, invoke your `init()` /\n    `main()` / `startSimulation()` function at the top level (either directly,\n    or inside a `window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ...)` handler).\n    Do NOT only attach it to `window.foo` and wait for something to call it.\n    If nothing runs at load time the page will be blank with zero errors,\n    which is the worst kind of failure to debug.\n\nH4. **ZERO CONSOLE ERRORS AT STARTUP.** Open the browser's DevTools console\n    mentally as you write: any `ReferenceError`, `TypeError`, `SyntaxError`,\n    or 404 on a script file at startup counts as a total failure. Code\n    defensively: before reading `someObject.someProp.someField`, make sure\n    `someObject.someProp` was actually assigned.\n\nH5. **NAMING CONTRACT — use these exact variable names, spelled exactly this way:**\n        - `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls` — top-level Three.js objects\n        - `elevatorCar` — the THREE.Group representing the elevator (NOT\n          `elevatorGroup`, NOT `lift`, NOT `car`). Doors and passengers are\n          added as children of `elevatorCar`.\n        - `elevatorCar.leftDoor` and `elevatorCar.rightDoor` — direct THREE.Mesh\n          references stored as properties on `elevatorCar` for animation access.\n        - `people` — array of person objects\n    A common past failure: the model created a local `elevatorGroup` inside\n    `createElevator()` but never assigned it to the global `elevatorCar`, then\n    later did `elevatorCar.leftDoor = ...` and crashed with\n    `Cannot set properties of undefined`. Either declare `elevatorCar` as a\n    global `let` and assign to it inside the factory, OR have the factory\n    `return` the group and do `elevatorCar = createElevator();` at the call site.\n\nH6. **CONSTANTS ARE TOP-LEVEL `const` DECLARATIONS — NOT wrapped in a config object.**\n    Write exactly this shape at the top of `elevator.js`:\n        ```\n        const FLOOR_HEIGHT = 3;\n        const FLOOR_COUNT = 6;\n        const BUILDING_WIDTH = 20;\n        const BUILDING_DEPTH = 15;\n        const SHAFT_WIDTH = 5;\n        const SHAFT_DEPTH = 5;\n        const ELEVATOR_SPEED = 2;\n        const PERSON_MOVE_SPEED = 1;\n        ```\n    Do NOT wrap them in `const config = { FLOOR_COUNT: 6, ... }`. A past failure\n    mixed styles — declared `config.FLOOR_COUNT` but then referenced bare\n    `FLOOR_COUNT` later, causing `ReferenceError: FLOOR_COUNT is not defined`.\n\nH7. **`person.userData` CONTRACT.** Every person object returned by\n    `createPerson()` in `person.js` must have this structure populated before\n    it is returned:\n        ```\n        person.userData = {\n            leftLeg:  <THREE.Object3D reference to the left leg mesh>,\n            rightLeg: <THREE.Object3D reference to the right leg mesh>,\n            isWalking: false\n        };\n        ```\n    The animation loop in `elevator.js` reads these fields every frame. If\n    `userData.leftLeg` is missing you will see hundreds of repeated\n    `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined` errors.\n\nH8. **REPARENTING PRESERVES WORLD TRANSFORM — USE `.attach()`, NOT `.add()`.**\n    This is the single most common visual bug and has affected nearly every\n    prior model. When you move the person between `scene` and `elevatorCar`,\n    you MUST use `THREE.Object3D.attach()`:\n        ```\n        // BOARDING (scene → elevator): preserves world position\n        elevatorCar.attach(person);\n\n        // EXITING (elevator → scene): preserves world position\n        scene.attach(person);\n        ```\n    Do NOT use `elevatorCar.add(person)` or `scene.add(person)` for reparenting\n    a person that already has a meaningful position in the world. `.add()`\n    keeps the raw `.position` numbers unchanged, which means the coordinate\n    frame silently switches underneath the object.\n\n    Concrete failure this rule prevents: after the elevator travels up, if you\n    do `scene.add(person)` while the person is a child of the elevator at\n    local `y = 0`, the person's `position.y` is still `0` but is now interpreted\n    in world coordinates — the passenger teleports down to the ground floor\n    (or walks out into mid-air above the roof in the reverse case).\n\n    If for some reason you cannot use `.attach()`, you must manually convert\n    before reparenting:\n        ```\n        const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();\n        person.getWorldPosition(worldPos);\n        elevatorCar.remove(person);\n        scene.add(person);\n        person.position.copy(worldPos);    // restore world-space position\n        ```\n    Do NOT try to \"fix it later\" by setting `person.position.y = floorY`\n    after the fact — that causes a visible teleport/snap.\n\n    Additional invariant: at any moment, the person's WORLD y-coordinate must\n    equal the Y of the floor they are currently standing on (or traveling\n    through inside the elevator). Sanity-check this: right before the exit\n    animation starts, `person.getWorldPosition(v).y` should equal the\n    destination floor's Y. After `scene.attach(person)`, it should still equal\n    that value.\n\n═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n## Core Requirements\n═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n### Visual Structure\n1. **Building**:\n   - Reset legs to standing position when stationary\n   - The legs should pivot from the hips/body, not the knees/mid-leg\n   - The animation loop reads `person.userData.leftLeg` / `rightLeg` / `isWalking`\n     — set `isWalking = true` when the person starts walking, `false` when they stop.\n\n6. **Door Animation**:\n   - Doors open BEFORE person enters/exits\n   - Doors close AFTER person is fully inside/outside\n   - Add brief delays (300ms) between steps for realism\n\nScene Graph & Parent-Child Relationships:\n  - When person boards the elevator, they must become a child of `elevatorCar`\n    (not the scene) so they travel with it during vertical movement.\n  - When the person exits, they must be re-parented back to `scene`.\n  - Elevator moves to pickup floor\n2. Doors open (sliding animation)\n3. Person walks forward into elevator (with leg animation) then becomes a child of elevator object\n4. Doors close\n5. Elevator travels to destination\n6. Doors open at destination\n7. Person walks forward to waiting spot (with leg animation) then is removed from elevator and added back to scene\n8. // The shin is the part that rotates during walking\n\n    // Right leg\n    const rightLegGroup = new THREE.Group();\n    const rightThigh = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(legRadius, legRadius * 0.9, legHeight / 2),\n        darkLegMaterial\n    );\n    rightThigh.position.y = legHeight / 4;\n    rightThigh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n    rightLegGroup.add(rightThigh);\n\n    const rightShin = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(legRadius * 0.9, legRadius * 0.85, legHeight / 2),\n        darkLegMaterial\n    );\n    rightShin.position.y = -legHeight / 4;\n    rightShin.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n    person.userData.rightLeg = rightShin; 3D Elevator Simulation\n */\n\n// ===== CONSTANTS =====\nconst FLOOR_HEIGHT = 3;\nconst FLOOR_COUNT = 6;\nconst BUILDING_WIDTH = 20;\nconst BUILDING_DEPTH = 15;\nconst SHAFT_WIDTH = 5;\nconst SHAFT_DEPTH = 5;\nconst ELEVATOR_SPEED = 2;\nconst PERSON_MOVE_SPEED = 1;\n\n// ===== GLOBAL STATE =====\nlet scene, camera, renderer, controls;\nlet elevatorCar;\nlet buildingGroup;\nlet people = [];\nlet animationSpeed = 5;\nlet isAnimating = false;\n\n// ===== BUILDING CREATION =====\nfunction createBuilding() {\n    const group = new THREE.Group();\n\n    // Floor material (transparent)\n    const floorMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xcccccc,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.3,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Wall material (semi-transparent)\n    const wallMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0x9999ff,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.2,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Calculate floor positions (ground at y=0, roof at y=FLOOR_COUNT*FLOOR_HEIGHT)\n    const groundY = 0;\n    const roofY = FLOOR_COUNT * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n    const floorSpacing = FLOOR_HEIGHT / (FLOOR_COUNT - 1);\n\n    // Create floors with shaft cutout\n    for (let i = 0; i < FLOOR_COUNT; i++) {\n        const y = groundY + i * floorSpacing;\n\n        // Floor plane (with shaft hole)\n        const floorGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(\n            BUILDING_WIDTH,\n            BUILDING_DEPTH\n        );\n        const floor = new THREE.Mesh(floorGeometry, floorMaterial);\n        floor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n        floor.position.set(0, y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + 0.1);\n        group.add(floor);\n\n        // Wall segments (skip shaft area in middle)\n        const halfShaftWidth = SHAFT_WIDTH / 2;\n        const wallThickness = 0.1;\n\n        // Front wall of this floor (at positive Z end)\n        const frontWallY = y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 + wallThickness / 2;\n        const backWallZ = -BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2;\n        \n        // Left wall segment\n        const leftWallX = -BUILDING_WIDTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2;\n        const frontLeftWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(BUILDING_DEPTH, FLOOR_HEIGHT, wallThickness),\n            wallMaterial\n        );\n        frontLeftWall.position.set(leftWallX, y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2);\n        group.add(frontLeftWall);\n\n        // Right wall segment\n        const rightWallX = BUILDING_WIDTH / 2 - wallThickness / 2;\n        const frontRightWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(BUILDING_DEPTH, FLOOR_HEIGHT, wallThickness),\n            wallMaterial\n        );\n        frontRightWall.position.set(rightWallX, y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2);\n        group.add(frontRightWall);\n\n        // Back wall segment (at negative Z end)\n        const backLeftWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(BUILDING_DEPTH, FLOOR_HEIGHT, wallThickness),\n            wallMaterial\n        );\n        backLeftWall.position.set(leftWallX, y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2, -BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2);\n        group.add(backLeftWall);\n\n        const backRightWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(BUILDING_DEPTH, FLOOR_HEIGHT, wallThickness),\n            wallMaterial\n        );\n        backRightWall.position.set(rightWallX, y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2, -BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2);\n        group.add(backRightWall);\n    }\n\n    // Solid ground floor (below all floors)\n    const groundMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0x888888,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n    const groundPlane = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(BUILDING_WIDTH, BUILDING_DEPTH),\n        groundMaterial\n    );\n    groundPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n    groundPlane.position.set(0, -FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 + 0.01, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + 0.1);\n    group.add(groundPlane);\n\n    // Solid roof (above all floors)\n    const roofMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0x888888,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n    const roofPlane = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(BUILDING_WIDTH, BUILDING_DEPTH),\n        roofMaterial\n    );\n    roofPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n    roofPlane.position.set(0, roofY + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 - 0.01, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + 0.1);\n    group.add(roofPlane);\n\n    // Add shaft cutout edges (visual guide)\n    const shaftColor = 0x555555;\n    const shaftEdgeMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n        color: shaftColor,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.15,\n        depthWrite: false\n    });\n    \n    // Draw edges of the shaft on each floor level\n    for (let i = 0; i < FLOOR_COUNT; i++) {\n        const y = groundY + i * floorSpacing;\n        const edgeHeight = 0.15;\n\n        const leftShaftEdge = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(SHAFT_WIDTH, edgeHeight, wallThickness),\n            shaftEdgeMaterial\n        );\n        leftShaftEdge.position.set(-SHAFT_WIDTH / 2 - SHAFT_DEPTH / 4, y + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 - edgeHeight / 2, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2);\n        group.add(leftShaftEdge);\n\n        const rightShaftEdge = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(SHAFT_WIDTH, edgeHeight, wallThickness),\n            shaftEdgeMaterial\n        );\n        rightShaftEdge.position.set(SHAFT_WIDTH / 2 + SHAFT_DEPTH / 4, y + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 - edgeHeight / 2, BUILDING_DEPTH / 2 + wallThickness / 2);\n        group.add(rightShaftEdge);\n\n        const frontShaftEdge = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(wallThickness, edgeHeight, SHAFT_DEPTH),\n            shaftEdgeMaterial\n        );\n        frontShaftEdge.position.set(-SHAFT_WIDTH / 2 - SHAFT_DEPTH / 4, y + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 - edgeHeight / 2, SHAFT_DEPTH / 2);\n        group.add(frontShaftEdge);\n\n        const backShaftEdge = new THREE.Mesh(\n            new THREE.BoxGeometry(wallThickness, edgeHeight, SHAFT_DEPTH),\n            shaftEdgeMaterial\n        );\n        backShaftEdge.position.set(-SHAFT_WIDTH / 2 - SHAFT_DEPTH / 4, y + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2 - edgeHeight / 2, -SHAFT_DEPTH / 2);\n        group.add(backShaftEdge);\n    }\n\n    return group;\n}\n\n// ===== ELEVATOR CREATION =====\nfunction createElevator() {\n    const car = new THREE.Group();\n\n    // Elevator frame material (yellow, semi-transparent)\n    const frameMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xffff00,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.5,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Solid back wall material\n    const backWallMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xeeeeee,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Transparent side walls\n    const sideWallMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xdddddd,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.4,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Inner floor material (solid for standing)\n    const innerFloorMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xf5f5f5,\n        depthWrite: false\n    });\n\n    // Door material (darker yellow, slightly more opaque than frame)\n    const doorMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xcccc00,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.7,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Car dimensions\n    const carWidth = 4;\n    const carDepth = 3;\n    const carHeight = 2.5;\n    const doorWidth = 1.8; // Each door is half the opening width\n\n    // === ELEVATOR FRAME ===\n\n    // Back wall (solid)\n    const backWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(carWidth, carHeight - 0.2, 0.3),\n        backWallMaterial\n    );\n    backWall.position.y = carHeight / 2 - 0.1;\n    car.add(backWall);\n\n    // Left side wall (transparent)\n    const leftSideWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, carHeight - 0.2, carDepth),\n        sideWallMaterial\n    );\n    leftSideWall.position.set(-carWidth / 2 + 0.15, carHeight / 2 - 0.1, 0);\n    car.add(leftSideWall);\n\n    // Right side wall (transparent)\n    const rightSideWall = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, carHeight - 0.2, carDepth),\n        sideWallMaterial\n    );\n    rightSideWall.position.set(carWidth / 2 - 0.15, carHeight / 2 - 0.1, 0);\n    car.add(rightSideWall);\n\n    // Inner floor (solid)\n    const innerFloor = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(carWidth - 0.6, carDepth - 0.4, 0.3),\n        innerFloorMaterial\n    );\n    innerFloor.position.y = -carHeight / 2 + 0.15;\n    car.add(innerFloor);\n\n    // === DOORS (sliding horizontally from center outward) ===\n    const doorHalfWidth = doorWidth / 2;\n    const doorDepth = 0.35; // Slightly less than back wall for proper depth sorting\n\n    // Left door - attached to left side of car frame, slides rightward to close\n    const leftDoorGroup = new THREE.Group();\n    const leftDoorMesh = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(doorWidth, carHeight - 0.25, doorDepth),\n        doorMaterial\n    );\n    // Position at the center of the opening (right half of elevator front)\n    // The opening is from -carWidth/2 to carWidth/2, so right half is from 0 to carWidth/2\n    leftDoorMesh.position.set(carHalfWidth - doorWidth / 2 + doorDepth / 2, carHeight / 2 - 0.125, carDepth / 2 - 0.1);\n    leftDoorGroup.add(leftDoorMesh);\n\n    // Right door - slides leftward to close\n    const rightDoorGroup = new THREE.Group();\n    const rightDoorMesh = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(doorWidth, carHeight - 0.25, doorDepth),\n        doorMaterial\n    );\n    // Position at the center of the opening (left half of elevator front)\n    leftDoorMesh.position.set(-carHalfWidth + doorWidth / 2 - doorDepth / 2, carHeight / 2 - 0.125, carDepth / 2 - 0.1);\n    rightDoorGroup.add(rightDoorMesh);\n\n    // Store references on the elevator group for animation access\n    car.leftDoor = leftDoorMesh;\n    car.rightDoor = rightDoorMesh;\n\n    // === ELEVATOR FRONT WALL (the frame around doors) ===\n    const frontFrameMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({\n        color: 0xffff00,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.5,\n        depthWrite: false,\n        side: THREE.DoubleSide\n    });\n\n    // Left frame piece (around left door)\n    const leftFrame = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(carHalfWidth - doorDepth, carHeight - 0.25, doorDepth * 1.5),\n        frontFrameMaterial\n    );\n    leftFrame.position.set(-carHalfWidth + doorDepth / 2 + doorWidth / 2, carHeight / 2 - 0.125, 0);\n    car.add(leftFrame);\n\n    // Right frame piece (around right door)\n    const rightFrame = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(carHalfWidth - doorDepth, carHeight - 0.25, doorDepth * 1.5),\n        frontFrameMaterial\n    );\n    rightFrame.position.set(carHalfWidth - doorDepth / 2 - doorWidth / 2, carHeight / 2 - 0.125, 0);\n    car.add(rightFrame);\n\n    // Center column between doors\n    const centerColumn = new THREE.Mesh(\n        new THREE.BoxGeometry(doorDepth * 1.5, carHeight - 0.25, doorDepth),\n        frontFrameMaterial\n    );\n    centerColumn.position.set(-doorWidth / 2 + doorDepth / 2, carHeight / 2 - 0.125, 0);\n    car.add(centerColumn);\n\n    // === POSITION AND INITIALIZE DOORS ===\n    const halfCarWidth = carWidth / 2;\n\n    // Set initial positions: doors are CLOSED (meeting in the middle)\n    // Left door's right edge should be at x=0, so:\n    // leftDoorMesh.position.x + leftDoorMesh.geometry.boundingSphere.radius <--- wait, let's recalculate\n    \n    // Actually, let me reconsider. The carWidth is 4, so halfCarWidth = 2.\n    // The opening is from x=-2 to x=2.\n    // Each door has width 1.8, so they each occupy from -0.9 to 0.9 when closed.\n    \n    // Left door center should be at x = -0.9 (so right edge is at x=0)\n    leftDoorMesh.position.x = -doorWidth / 2;\n\n    // Right door center should be at x = +0.9 (so left edge is at x=0)\n    rightDoorMesh.position.x = doorWidth / 2;\n\n    // Store initial closed positions for reference\n    const CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET = doorWidth / 2; // Each door extends from its center by half its width\n\n    return {\n        group: car,\n        frontOffset: -carDepth / 2,\n        backOffset: carDepth / 2\n    };\n}\n\n// ===== SIMULATION LOGIC =====\n\n/**\n * Selects a random person from the people array and returns them.\n * @returns {THREE.Group|null} A person object or null if no one is available.\n */\nfunction selectPerson() {\n    if (people.length === 0) return null;\n    const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * people.length);\n    return people.splice(idx, 1)[0];\n}\n\n/**\n * Moves a person to the specified floor and returns a promise that resolves when done.\n */\nfunction movePersonToFloor(person, targetFloor) {\n    // Target floor Y coordinate (floor 0 is at y=0)\n    const targetY = (targetFloor - 1) * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n\n    return new Promise((resolve) => {\n        if (person.parent === elevatorCar && elevatorCar.position.y !== targetY + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2) {\n            // Elevator needs to move first, but we handle that in the main loop\n            return resolve();\n        }\n\n        const isInside = person.parent === elevatorCar;\n\n        if (isInside) {\n            // Person is inside the elevator, walking forward through open doors\n            let positionZ = 0; // Starting at door opening position\n            const maxPositionZ = 1.5; // Distance to wait in front of elevator\n\n            function step() {\n                if (!person.parent || person.parent !== elevatorCar) {\n                    resolve();\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                positionZ += PERSON_MOVE_SPEED * (1 / animationSpeed);\n                person.position.z = Math.min(positionZ, maxPositionZ);\n\n                // Check if fully exited\n                if (positionZ >= maxPositionZ - 0.01) {\n                    resolve();\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                requestAnimationFrame(step);\n            }\n            step();\n        } else {\n            // Person is outside, walking forward into elevator\n            let positionZ = 2; // Starting at waiting area in front of doors\n            const maxPositionZ = -1.5; // Door opening position (negative Z)\n\n            function step() {\n                if (!person.parent || person.parent !== scene) {\n                    resolve();\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                positionZ -= PERSON_MOVE_SPEED * (1 / animationSpeed);\n                person.position.z = Math.max(positionZ, maxPositionZ);\n\n                // Check if fully entered\n                if (positionZ <= maxPositionZ + 0.01) {\n                    resolve();\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                requestAnimationFrame(step);\n            }\n            step();\n        }\n    });\n}\n\n/**\n * Handles door opening animation.\n */\nasync function openDoors() {\n    const car = elevatorCar.group;\n    if (!car) return true;\n\n    // Doors slide outward from center (positive X direction for left, negative for right)\n    const targetOpenX = CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET + 1.0; // Open beyond frame\n\n    let leftDone = false;\n    let rightDone = false;\n\n    return new Promise((resolve) => {\n        function animateLeftDoor() {\n            if (!leftDone && car.leftDoor) {\n                const currentX = car.leftDoor.position.x;\n                const newX = currentX + (targetOpenX - currentX) * 0.15;\n                car.leftDoor.position.x = newX;\n\n                if (Math.abs(newX - targetOpenX) < 0.01) {\n                    car.leftDoor.position.x = targetOpenX;\n                    leftDone = true;\n                    // Check right door too\n                    if (rightDone) resolve();\n                    else animateRightDoor();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        function animateRightDoor() {\n            if (!rightDone && car.rightDoor) {\n                const currentX = car.rightDoor.position.x;\n                const newX = currentX + (targetOpenX - currentX) * 0.15;\n                car.rightDoor.position.x = newX;\n\n                if (Math.abs(newX - targetOpenX) < 0.01) {\n                    car.rightDoor.position.x = targetOpenX;\n                    rightDone = true;\n                    // Left door should already be done, resolve\n                    resolve();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        animateLeftDoor();\n    });\n}\n\n/**\n * Handles door closing animation.\n */\nasync function closeDoors() {\n    const car = elevatorCar.group;\n    if (!car) return true;\n\n    // Doors slide inward to center (negative X for left, positive for right)\n    let leftDone = false;\n    let rightDone = false;\n\n    return new Promise((resolve) => {\n        function animateLeftDoor() {\n            if (!leftDone && car.leftDoor) {\n                const currentX = car.leftDoor.position.x;\n                const newX = currentX + (-CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET - currentX) * 0.15;\n                car.leftDoor.position.x = newX;\n\n                if (Math.abs(newX - (-CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET)) < 0.01) {\n                    car.leftDoor.position.x = -CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET;\n                    leftDone = true;\n                    if (rightDone) resolve();\n                    else animateRightDoor();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        function animateRightDoor() {\n            if (!rightDone && car.rightDoor) {\n                const currentX = car.rightDoor.position.x;\n                const newX = currentX + (CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET - currentX) * 0.15;\n                car.rightDoor.position.x = newX;\n\n                if (Math.abs(newX - CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET) < 0.01) {\n                    car.rightDoor.position.x = CLOSED_DOOR_X_OFFSET;\n                    rightDone = true;\n                    resolve();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        animateLeftDoor();\n    });\n}\n\n/**\n * Main simulation loop.\n */\nfunction simulate() {\n    // Pick a random person to move\n    const personToMove = selectPerson();\n\n    if (!personToMove) return;\n\n    // Select a random floor for the destination (excluding current floor)\n    let destFloorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * FLOOR_COUNT);\n    do {\n        destFloorIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * FLOOR_COUNT);\n    } while (destFloorIdx === 0); // Floor 0 is ground, no one goes down there\n\n    const destFloorY = (destFloorIdx - 1) * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n\n    // Sequence:\n    // 1. Elevator moves to pickup floor\n    // 2. Doors open\n    // 3. Person boards/exits\n    // 4. Doors close\n    // 5. Elevator moves to destination\n\n    let step = 0;\n\n    function nextStep() {\n        if (step === 0) {\n            // Step 1: Move elevator to pickup floor\n            const currentY = elevatorCar.position.y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2;\n            const targetY = destFloorY + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2;\n            const deltaY = targetY - currentY;\n\n            if (Math.abs(deltaY) < ELEVATOR_SPEED * (1 / animationSpeed)) {\n                elevatorCar.position.y = targetY;\n                step = 1;\n            } else {\n                elevatorCar.position.y += Math.sign(deltaY) * ELEVATOR_SPEED * (1 / animationSpeed);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (step === 1) {\n            // Step 2: Open doors\n            openDoors().then(() => {\n                step = 2;\n                nextStep();\n            });\n        }\n\n        if (step === 2) {\n            // Step 3: Person walks through door and boards/exits\n            movePersonToFloor(personToMove, destFloorIdx).then(() => {\n                personToMove.position.z = -1.5; // Fully at door position\n\n                // Re-parent the person based on location\n                if (personToMove.parent === scene) {\n                    elevatorCar.attach(personToMove);\n                }\n\n                step = 3;\n                nextStep();\n            });\n        }\n\n        if (step === 3) {\n            // Step 4: Close doors\n            closeDoors().then(() => {\n                step = 4;\n                nextStep();\n            });\n        }\n\n        if (step === 4) {\n            // Step 5: Move elevator to destination\n            const currentY = elevatorCar.position.y - FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2;\n            const targetY = destFloorY + FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2;\n            const deltaY = targetY - currentY;\n\n            if (Math.abs(deltaY) < ELEVATOR_SPEED * (1 / animationSpeed)) {\n                elevatorCar.position.y = targetY;\n                // Next cycle, select a new person\n                step = 0;\n                nextStep();\n            } else {\n                elevatorCar.position.y += Math.sign(deltaY) * ELEVATOR_SPEED * (1 / animationSpeed);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    nextStep();\n}\n\n/**\n * Animation loop.\n */\nfunction animate() {\n    requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n    // Update speed slider\n    const sliderValue = parseInt(document.getElementById('speedSlider').value) || 5;\n    animationSpeed = Math.max(1, sliderValue);\n    document.getElementById('speedValue').textContent = 'x' + animationSpeed;\n\n    renderer.render(scene, camera);\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialization - called when page loads.\n */\nfunction init() {\n    // Setup scene\n    scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    scene.background = null; // Transparent background\n\n    // Camera setup\n    camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 1, 1000);\n    camera.position.set(25, 25, 25);\n    camera.lookAt(0, (FLOOR_COUNT - 1) * FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2, 0);\n\n    // WebGL renderer with transparency enabled\n    renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({\n        antialias: true,\n        alpha: true\n    });\n    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\n    renderer.sortObjects = true;\n    renderer.alpha = true;\n    document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n    // OrbitControls\n    controls = new THREE.OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);\n    controls.enableDamping = true;\n\n    // Create building\n    buildingGroup = createBuilding();\n    scene.add(buildingGroup);\n\n    // Elevator starts at ground level (floor 1)\n    elevatorCar = createElevator().group;\n    elevatorCar.position.y = FLOOR_HEIGHT / 2; // Ground floor is at y=0, so half height centers it\n    buildingGroup.add(elevatorCar);\n\n    // Create some initial people waiting in front of the elevator\n    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n        const person = createPerson();\n        // Position in front of elevator at ground level\n        person.position.set(-5, 0.6, 6);\n        scene.add(person);\n        people.push(person);\n    }\n\n    // Handle window resize\n    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {\n        camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\n        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\n    });\n\n    // Start animation loop and simulation\n    animate();\n    simulate();\n}\n\n// Auto-start on page load (H3 requirement)\nif (document.readyState === 'loading') {\n    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n} else {\n    init();\n}","filePath":"/home/jeff/Code/llm-eval/evals/opencode_qwen3_5-9b-glm5_1-distill-v1_elevator_prompt_v2/elevator.js"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/home/jeff/Code/llm-eval/evals/opencode_qwen3_5-9b-glm5_1-distill-v1_elevator_prompt_v2/elevator.js","exists":false,"truncated":false},"title":"evals/opencode_qwen3_5-9b-glm5_1-distill-v1_elevator_prompt_v2/elevator.js","time":{"start":1778989697183,"end":1778989697196}},"id":"prt_e34037a59001Ec3sB9zlZLA7W5","sessionID":"ses_1cc027726ffeD86Pmw2cTpD9o0","messageID":"msg_e340324d5001tuLMJEzNYmiHa4"}}
